That pattern confirms the theory: the ruby was on the hard readings. UPDATED as of chunk 11: five more, same pattern, all on hard or uncommon readings — 「燭 台」 (00000E19:11:1), 「 懐 」 read ふところ (00000E01:11:74), 「 呟 いていた」 (00000E49:11:110), 「 絆 」 (00000E61:11:118), 「 遮 っている」 (00000E61:45:13). Leading-space-only cases are now as common as surrounding-space cases. The killer's costume is therefore funeral dress, not a disguise.
